     1	// In-memory fs fake for unit tests. Synchronous subset only.
     2	// Atomicity contract: renameSync moves content in a single step, so a target
     3	// path is never observed carrying partial content. Every mutation appends a
     4	// full snapshot to `fs.__history`, letting tests enumerate intermediate states
     5	// and prove the tmp+rename discipline (target's first appearance is a rename).
     6	
     7	function fsError(code, syscall, path) {
     8	  const e = new Error(`${code}: ${syscall} '${path}'`);
     9	  e.code = code;
    10	  e.syscall = syscall;
    11	  e.path = path;
    12	  return e;
    13	}
    14	
    15	function normalize(p) {
    16	  const str = String(p);
    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
    18	  const out = [];
    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
    21	    if (part === '..') {
    22	      out.pop();
    23	      continue;
    24	    }
    25	    out.push(part);
    26	  }
    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
    28	}
    29	
    30	function dirname(np) {
    31	  const i = np.lastIndexOf('/');
    32	  if (i <= 0) return '/';
    33	  return np.slice(0, i);
    34	}
    35	
    36	function basename(np, parent) {
    37	  return parent === '/' ? np.slice(1) : np.slice(parent.length + 1);
    38	}
    39	
    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
    44	  const history = [];
    45	  let clock = 0;
    46	
    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
    50	
    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
    52	    let cur = '';
    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
    55	      dirs.add(cur);
    56	    }
    57	  };
    58	
    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
    63	  };
    64	
    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
    66	    const np = normalize(p);
    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
    69	    touch(np);
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  const fs = {
    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
    74	      const np = normalize(p);
    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
    76	      return store.get(np);
    77	    },
    78	
    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
    80	      const np = normalize(p);
    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
    83	      touch(np);
    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
    85	    },
    86	
    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
    88	      const np = normalize(p);
    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
    91	      touch(np);
    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
    93	    },
    94	
    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
   101	        store.delete(nf);
   102	        store.set(nt, content);
   103	        touch(nt);
   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
   105	        return;
   106	      }
   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
   113	        dirs.add(nt);
   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
   117	            store.delete(k);
   118	          }
   119	        }
   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
   123	            dirs.delete(d);
   124	          }
   125	        }
   126	        touch(nt);
   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
   128	        return;
   129	      }
   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
   131	    },
   132	
   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
   134	      const np = normalize(p);
   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
   136	        mkdirp(np);
   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
   138	        return undefined;
   139	      }
   140	      ensureParent(np, 'mkdir');
   141	      if (dirs.has(np) || store.has(np)) throw fsError('EEXIST', 'mkdir', np);
   142	      dirs.add(np);
   143	      record('mkdirSync', [np]);
   144	      return undefined;
   145	    },
   146	
   147	    existsSync(p) {
   148	      const np = normalize(p);
   149	      return store.has(np) || dirs.has(np);
   150	    },
   151	
   152	    readdirSync(p) {
   153	      const np = normalize(p);
   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {
   155	        if (store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOTDIR', 'scandir', np);
   156	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'scandir', np);
   157	      }
   158	      const names = new Set();
   159	      const collect = (k) => {
   160	        if (k !== np && dirname(k) === np) names.add(basename(k, np));
   161	      };
   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
   164	      return [...names];
   165	    },
   166	
   167	    unlinkSync(p) {
   168	      const np = normalize(p);
   169	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'unlink', np);
   170	      store.delete(np);
   171	      record('unlinkSync', [np]);
   172	    },
   173	
   174	    rmSync(p, opts = {}) {
   175	      const np = normalize(p);
   176	      const isFile = store.has(np);
   177	      const isDir = dirs.has(np);
   178	      if (!isFile && !isDir) {
   179	        if (opts.force) return;
   180	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rm', np);
   181	      }
   182	      if (isFile) store.delete(np);
   183	      if (isDir) {
   184	        const pre = np + '/';
   185	        if (!opts.recursive) {
   186	          const hasChildren = [...store.keys(), ...dirs].some((k) => k !== np && k.startsWith(pre));
   187	          if (hasChildren) throw fsError('ENOTEMPTY', 'rmdir', np);
   188	          dirs.delete(np);
   189	        } else {
   190	          for (const k of [...store.keys()]) if (k === np || k.startsWith(pre)) store.delete(k);
   191	          for (const d of [...dirs]) if (d === np || d.startsWith(pre)) dirs.delete(d);
   192	        }
   193	      }
   194	      record('rmSync', [np]);
   195	    },
   196	
   197	    statSync(p) {
   198	      const np = normalize(p);
   199	      if (store.has(np)) {
   200	        const size = Buffer.byteLength(store.get(np));
   201	        return { isFile: () => true, isDirectory: () => false, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
   202	      }
   203	      if (dirs.has(np)) {
   204	        return { isFile: () => false, isDirectory: () => true, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size: 0, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
   205	      }
   206	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'stat', np);
   207	    },
   208	
   209	    // The fake cannot represent symlinks, so lstat === stat and realpath is
   210	    // normalization. Symlink-refusal behavior is proven over the real fs in
   211	    // tests/integration/symlink-escape.test.mjs.
   212	    lstatSync(p) {
   213	      return fs.statSync(p);
   214	    },
   215	
   216	    realpathSync(p) {
   217	      const np = normalize(p);
   218	      if (!store.has(np) && !dirs.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'realpath', np);
   219	      return np;
   220	    },
   221	
   222	    copyFileSync(src, dest) {
   223	      const ns = normalize(src);
   224	      const nd = normalize(dest);
   225	      if (!store.has(ns)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'copyfile', ns);
   226	      ensureParent(nd, 'copyfile');
   227	      store.set(nd, store.get(ns));
   228	      touch(nd);
   229	      record('copyFileSync', [ns, nd]);
   230	    },
   231	  };
   232	
   233	  // Introspection hook for atomicity assertions; not part of the real fs API.
   234	  fs.__history = history;
   235	
   236	  return { fs, files: () => snapshot() };
   237	}

     8	/** @param {string} path */
     9	function tmpPathFor(path) {
    10	  tmpCounter += 1;
    11	  return `${path}.tmp.${process.pid}-${tmpCounter}`;
    12	}
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * Rename with retry on EPERM (Windows AV/indexer lock pattern): 3 attempts total.
    16	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} from @param {string} to
    17	 */
    18	function renameWithRetry(fs, from, to) {
    19	  let lastErr;
    20	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
    21	    try {
    22	      fs.renameSync(from, to);
    23	      return;
    24	    } catch (err) {
    25	      lastErr = err;
    26	      if (!err || /** @type {any} */ (err).code !== 'EPERM') throw err;
    27	    }
    28	  }
    29	  throw lastErr;
    30	}
    31	
    32	/**
    33	 * Write text atomically: tmp sibling + rename. The live target is never
    34	 * truncated or partially written.
    35	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    36	 */
    37	export function atomicWriteText(fs, path, content) {
    38	  const tmp = tmpPathFor(path);
    39	  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, content);
    40	  try {
    41	    renameWithRetry(fs, tmp, path);
    42	  } catch (err) {
    43	    try {
    44	      fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
    45	    } catch {
    46	      // leftover tmp is harmless; cleanupTmp removes it later
    47	    }
    48	    throw err;
    49	  }
    50	}
    51	
    52	/**
    53	 * Atomically write pretty-printed JSON (2-space, trailing newline).
    54	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    55	 */
    56	export function atomicWriteJson(fs, path, obj) {
    57	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) + '\n');
    58	}
    59	
    60	/**
    61	 * Read + parse JSON without ever throwing.
    62	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    63	 * @returns {{ok: true, value: any} | {ok: false, error: string}}
    64	 */
    65	export function safeReadJson(fs, path) {
    66	  let raw;
    67	  try {
    68	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    69	  } catch (err) {
    70	    return { ok: false, error: `read failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code ?? err}` };
    71	  }
    72	  try {
    73	    return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(raw) };
    74	  } catch (err) {
    75	    return { ok: false, error: `parse failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err}` };
    76	  }
    77	}

    79	/**
    80	 * Copy the current content to <path>.bak (when present), then write atomically.
    81	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    82	 */
    83	export function backupThenWrite(fs, path, content) {
    84	  if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
    85	    fs.copyFileSync(path, `${path}.bak`);
    86	  }
    87	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, content);
    88	}

    90	/**
    91	 * Remove leftover *.tmp.* files in a directory (crash debris).
    92	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} dir
    93	 */
    94	export function cleanupTmp(fs, dir) {
    95	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
    96	  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    97	    if (/\.tmp\./.test(name)) {
    98	      try {
    99	        fs.unlinkSync(`${dir}/${name}`);
   100	      } catch {
   101	        // already gone or locked; not fatal
   102	      }
   103	    }
   104	  }
   105	}

    40	/**
    41	 * Structural validation, deep (gate-2 fix 6): bundles are untrusted input, so
    42	 * every nested shape that render or the commands consume is checked — a string
    43	 * task.constraints must fail here, not crash renderHandoffMd — and file paths
    44	 * are jailed to the repository. Accumulates one {path, msg} per problem;
    45	 * unknown extra fields are tolerated everywhere (forward compatibility).
    46	 * @param {any} obj
    47	 * @returns {{ok: boolean, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
    48	 */
    49	export function validateBundle(obj) {
    50	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    51	  const errors = [];
    52	  const err = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ msg) => errors.push({ path, msg });
    53	
    54	  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') {
    55	    return { ok: false, errors: [{ path: '', msg: 'bundle must be an object' }] };
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (obj.schema !== BUNDLE_SCHEMA) err('schema', `expected "${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}", got ${JSON.stringify(obj.schema)}`);
    59	  if (typeof obj.bundleId !== 'string') err('bundleId', 'must be a string');
    60	  if (typeof obj.generation !== 'number') err('generation', 'must be a number');
    61	  if (typeof obj.createdAt !== 'string') err('createdAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    62	  if (typeof obj.updatedAt !== 'string') err('updatedAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    63	  if (typeof obj.journalSeq !== 'number') err('journalSeq', 'must be a number');
    64	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.decisions)) {
    65	    err('decisions', 'must be an array');
    66	  } else {
    67	    obj.decisions.forEach((/** @type {any} */ d, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    68	      if (d === null || typeof d !== 'object') err(`decisions[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
    69	    });
    70	  }
    71	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.dedupeRing)) err('dedupeRing', 'must be an array');
    72	
    73	  if (obj.origin === null || typeof obj.origin !== 'object') {
    74	    err('origin', 'must be an object');
    75	  } else if (typeof obj.origin.platform !== 'string') {
    76	    err('origin.platform', 'must be a string');
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  if (obj.task === null || typeof obj.task !== 'object') {
    80	    err('task', 'must be an object');

    82	    if (typeof obj.task.goal !== 'string') err('task.goal', 'must be a string');
    83	    if (!Array.isArray(obj.task.constraints)) {
    84	      err('task.constraints', 'must be an array of strings');

    88	      });
    89	    }

    91	  }
    92

    94	    err('plan', 'must be an object');
    95	  } else if (!Array.isArray(obj.plan.steps)) {
    96	    err('plan.steps', 'must be an array');
    97	  } else {
    98	    obj.plan.steps.forEach((/** @type {any} */ s, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    99	      if (s === null || typeof s !== 'object') err(`plan.steps[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
   100	    });
   101	  }
   102

   119	    err('roles.assignments', 'must be an object');

   147	/**
   148	 * Version migration seam. Schema v1 is identity; anything else is unsupported.
   149	 * @param {any} obj

   151	export function migrate(obj) {
   152	  if (obj?.schema === BUNDLE_SCHEMA) return structuredClone(obj);
   153	  throw new Error(`unsupported bundle schema ${JSON.stringify(obj?.schema)}; this baton understands ${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}`);
   154	}

     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { resolveRoles } from '../../core/src/roles/resolve.mjs';
     4	
     5	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     6	// RED — resolver entry-level avoidance (subtask l1-resolver-entries, part A).
     7	//
     8	// Source of truth: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md
     9	//   §"Model-level failover (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 4)" and
    10	//   §"Acceptance constraints" constraint 2; docs/design/core.md §Module APIs
    11	//   (resolveRoles). Mirrors the idioms in tests/unit/resolve.test.mjs.
    12	//
    13	// CONTRACT (pinned here):
    14	//   A1. NEW OPT. resolveRoles opts gain `avoidEntries: [{platform, model}]`,
    15	//       alongside the existing platform-level `avoid[]`. A chain entry is
    16	//       skipped by this rule ONLY when BOTH platform AND model match an
    17	//       avoidEntries item — a different model on the same platform stays
    18	//       selectable (that is the whole point: model-level, not platform-level).
    19	//   A2. AUDIT. An entry-avoided skip is recorded in skipped[] with a DISTINCT
    20	//       why: 'avoided-entry' (mirrors the existing skip-recording idiom:
    21	//       {chainIndex, platform, model, why}). The platform-level rule keeps its
    22	//       own why 'avoided' — the two are never conflated.
    23	//   A3. NAMED TEST (constraint 2). A chain whose first entry is codex/<A> and
    24	//       second codex/<B>, with avoidEntries [{platform:'codex', model:'<A>'}],
    25	//       selects <B> on the SAME platform (mode stays native), with no
    26	//       platform-wide avoidance.
    27	//   A4. COMPOSITION. avoidEntries composes with avoid[]: an entry on an AVOIDED
    28	//       PLATFORM is skipped by the platform rule (why 'avoided'); entry
    29	//       avoidance never resurrects a platform-avoided entry.
    30	//   A5. EFFORT SUFFIX. Config chain strings are "platform/model[@effort]"
    31	//       (matrix.mjs parses model + effort apart). avoidEntries matches on MODEL
    32	//       IDENTITY regardless of any @effort suffix — model is what's
    33	//       unavailable, not a specific effort. So an avoidEntries model carrying an
    34	//       @effort suffix matches a parsed entry of the same base model, and effort
    35	//       differences never prevent (or force) a match.
    36	//   A6. EXHAUSTION. All entries avoided → the existing 'unavailable' path fires
    37	//       (platform/model null, full skipped[]), never a crash.
    38	//   A7. BACKWARD COMPAT. Omitted OR empty avoidEntries → behavior identical to
    39	//       today (regression negatives, GREEN both today and after).
    40	//
    41	// PROOF-OF-RED: avoidEntries does not exist yet, so resolveRoles ignores it and
    42	// selects the FIRST platform-eligible entry. The RED tests assert the
    43	// entry-avoided selection / skip audit, which fails today (the avoided entry is
    44	// still chosen). Tests labeled GREEN are invariants that hold both today and
    45	// after and guard against over-broad matching.
    46	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47

    49	const PLATFORMS = { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} };
    50	const DEFAULTS = { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' };
    51	const oneRole = (/** @type {any[]} */ chain) => ({ schema: 'baton/config@1', roles: { r: chain }, platforms: { ...PLATFORMS }, defaults: { ...DEFAULTS } });
    52

    58	      to: 'codex',
    59	      avoidEntries: [{ platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.5' }],
    60	    });

    63	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'native', 'mode stays native (no platform failover)');

     1	const PRECEDENCE = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'model-unavailable', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     2	
     3	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
    10	};
    11	
    12	// eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}

     5	/** Audit note for a refused payload — visible in the decision log, never a throw. */
     6	const refusalNote = (/** @type {string} */ type, /** @type {any} */ detail) =>
     7	  `[${type} with invalid payload refused: ${JSON.stringify(detail).slice(0, 120)}]`;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Pure event reducer. Returns a new bundle; never mutates the input.
    11	 * Idempotent: an event whose dedupeKey is still in the bundle's ring is a no-op.
    12	 * Unknown event types degrade to a decision-log note — a checkpoint must never
    13	 * fail because a newer adapter emitted something this core doesn't know — and
    14	 * journal payloads are untrusted (gate-2 fix 6): malformed shapes degrade to a
    15	 * refusal note and repository paths are jailed before storage.
    16	 * @param {any} bundle
    17	 * @param {{seq: number, ts: string, type: string, dedupeKey: string, writerId?: string, source?: string, payload: any}} event
    18	 */
    19	export function applyEvent(bundle, event) {
    20	  // Journal lines are untrusted (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable null/number/
    21	  // string is a valid JSON line but not an event envelope — degrade to a
    22	  // no-op rather than crash replay. The store's load loop also pre-filters and
    23	  // warns; this is the last-line guard for any direct caller.
    24	  if (event === null || typeof event !== 'object' || Array.isArray(event)) return bundle;
    25	  if (bundle.dedupeRing.includes(event.dedupeKey)) return bundle;
    26	
    27	  const out = structuredClone(bundle);
    28	  const { seq, ts, type, payload = {} } = structuredClone(event);
    29	
    30	  switch (type) {
    31	    case 'decision': {
    32	      /** @type {{seq: number, ts: string, summary: any, detail?: any}} */
    33	      const entry = { seq, ts, summary: payload.summary };
    34	      if (payload.detail !== undefined) entry.detail = payload.detail;
    35	      out.decisions.push(entry);
    36	      break;
    37	    }
    38	    case 'note':
    39	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: payload.text });
    40	      // A structured StopFailure rate_limit note marks the open bundle
    41	      // limit-hit (gate-2 fix 3): the unsealed limit death must be visible to
    42	      // the next SessionStart on any platform, not buried in the decision log.
    43	      if (payload.structured?.kind === 'stop-failure' && payload.errorType === 'rate_limit' && out.handoff.status === 'open') {
    44	        out.handoff = { ...out.handoff, reason: out.handoff.reason ?? payload.text ?? null, reasonClass: 'usage-limit' };
    45	      }
    46	      break;
    47	    case 'plan.set':
    48	      if (Array.isArray(payload.steps) && payload.steps.every((/** @type {any} */ s) => s !== null && typeof s === 'object')) {
    49	        out.plan.steps = payload.steps;
    50	      } else {
    51	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.set', payload?.steps) });
    52	      }
    53	      break;
    54	    case 'plan.step': {
    55	      if (payload === null || typeof payload !== 'object' || typeof payload.id !== 'string') {
    56	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.step', payload) });
    57	        break;
    58	      }
    59	      const existing = out.plan.steps.find((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id === payload.id);
    60	      if (existing) Object.assign(existing, payload);
    61	      else out.plan.steps.push(payload);
    62	      break;
    63	    }
    64	    case 'file.touch': {
    65	      const safePath = jailRelPath(payload?.path);
    66	      if (safePath === null) {
    67	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('file.touch', payload?.path) });
    68	        break;
    69	      }
    70	      const entry = { path: safePath, op: typeof payload.op === 'string' ? payload.op : 'touch', lastTs: ts };
    71	      const i = out.files.touched.findIndex((/** @type {any} */ f) => f.path === safePath);
    72	      if (i === -1) out.files.touched.push(entry);
    73	      else out.files.touched[i] = entry;
    74	      break;
    75	    }
    76	    case 'roles.remap': {
    77	      // The assignments object AND every value must be non-null objects (iter-3
    78	      // F3): a well-formed {planner: null} used to be stored verbatim and then
    79	      // crashed renderHandoffMd. Untrusted journal input degrades to a note.
    80	      const a = payload?.assignments;
    81	      const wellFormed =
    82	        a !== null &&
    83	        typeof a === 'object' &&
    84	        !Array.isArray(a) &&
    85	        Object.values(a).every((/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v));
    86	      if (wellFormed) {
    87	        out.roles.assignments = a;
    88	      } else {
    89	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('roles.remap', a) });
    90	      }
    91	      break;
    92	    }
    93	    case 'task.update': {
    94	      /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    95	      const patch = {};
    96	      if (typeof payload?.goal === 'string') patch.goal = payload.goal;
    97	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.constraints) && payload.constraints.every((/** @type {any} */ c) => typeof c === 'string')) {
    98	        patch.constraints = payload.constraints;
    99	      }
   100	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.acceptance)) patch.acceptance = payload.acceptance;
   101	      if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
   102	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('task.update', payload) });
   103	        break;
   104	      }
   105	      out.task = { ...out.task, ...patch };
   106	      break;
   107	    }
   108	    case 'git.update':
   109	      if (payload === null || typeof payload === 'object') out.git = payload;
   110	      else out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('git.update', payload) });
   111	      break;
   112	    case 'bundle.seed':
   113	      // Self-applying identity (gate-2 iter-2 B6): the auto-seed emits this as
   114	      // the journal's first event so a journal-only rebuild (snapshot + .bak
   115	      // both gone) restores the bundle's identity/origin/task instead of the
   116	      // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. Replayed first (lowest seq),
   117	      // so later events layer on top; on normal replay its seq is below the
   118	      // snapshot's journalSeq and it is skipped.
   119	      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
   120	        if (typeof payload.bundleId === 'string') out.bundleId = payload.bundleId;
   121	        if (typeof payload.generation === 'number') out.generation = payload.generation;
   122	        if (typeof payload.createdAt === 'string') out.createdAt = payload.createdAt;
   123	        // Restore identity ONLY (platform/model) — never sessionHint/unstable.
   124	        // Session ownership is adopted AFTER the seed (a snapshot-only mutation),
   125	        // so replaying the seed must not clobber a later-adopted hint back to
   126	        // its seed-time null (gate-2 iter-2: caught by the adoption test).
   127	        if (payload.origin && typeof payload.origin === 'object') {
   128	          if (typeof payload.origin.platform === 'string') out.origin.platform = payload.origin.platform;
   129	          if (typeof payload.origin.model === 'string') out.origin.model = payload.origin.model;
   130	        }
   131	        if (payload.task && typeof payload.task === 'object' && typeof payload.task.goal === 'string') {
   132	          out.task.goal = payload.task.goal;
   133	        }
   134	      }
   135	      break;
   136	    case 'transcript.set':
   137	      // Opt-in transcript tail (plan §Transcript policy): stored under the
   138	      // literal property name `transcript` so purge-transcript strips it from
   139	      // every retained copy. Render never includes it.
   140	      if (payload?.transcript !== null && typeof payload?.transcript === 'object' && typeof payload.transcript.tail === 'string') {
   141	        out.transcript = {
   142	          capturedAt: typeof payload.transcript.capturedAt === 'string' ? payload.transcript.capturedAt : ts,
   143	          tail: payload.transcript.tail,

   146	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('transcript.set', 'malformed payload') });
   147	      }

   149	    default:
   150	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: `[unhandled event type '${type}' recorded as note]` });
   151	      break;
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  out.updatedAt = ts;
   155	  // Harden against foreign/keyless entries (gate-2 fix): a missing seq must
   156	  // never poison journalSeq to NaN, and an absent dedupeKey must never enter
   157	  // the ring (undefined in the ring dedupes every later keyless event).

   162	  }
   163	
   164	  return out;
   165	}

   157	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
   158	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
   159	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
   160	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
   161	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   162	 */
   163	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
   164	  if (isSupervisedChild(io)) return 0;
   165	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', model: 'string', trigger: 'string', debounce: 'string', session: 'string', strict: 'boolean', 'take-over': 'boolean' });
   166	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'checkpoint', parsed.error);
   167	  const flags = parsed.flags;
   168	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   169	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
   170	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
   171	  const pErr = platformError(platform);
   172	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', pErr);
   173	  // A supervisor passing --session must never silently fall back to
   174	  // payload-derived identity: an empty value is a hard usage error.
   175	  if (flags.session === '') return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--session must be a non-empty session hint (it cannot be empty)');
   176	
   177	  try {
   178	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
   179	  } catch (err) {
   180	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   181	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
   182	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
   183	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
   184	  }
   185	}
   186	
   187	/**
   188	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   189	 * @param {string} platform
   190	 * @param {any} io
   191	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   192	 */
   193	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   194	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   195	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   196	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   197	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   198	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   199	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   200	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   201	    return code;
   202	  };
   203	
   204	  const parsed = parseStdin(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   205	  if (!parsed.ok) {
   206	    const why = parsed.why ?? 'send one JSON value ({"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}), a JSON array of events, or NDJSON event lines';
   207	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed — ${why}\n`);
   208	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: why } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   209	  }
   210	  const raw = parsed.raw;
   211	
   212	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   213	  // The hook command declares which event it is (--trigger), so event identity
   214	  // never depends on parsing each harness's payload shape (Cursor's stop payload
   215	  // names the event nowhere normalize could find it).
   216	  const explicitEvent = typeof flags.trigger === 'string' ? flags.trigger : undefined;
   217	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent);
   218	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   219	
   220	  // --session: the caller (the loop supervisor) checkpoints under its OWN
   221	  // stable identity — every event's hint is overridden, so ownership,
   222	  // adoption, foreign checks, and journal writerIds all key on the flag,
   223	  // never on whatever session id the piped payload happens to carry.
   224	  if (typeof flags.session === 'string') {
   225	    for (const ev of events) {
   226	      ev.sessionHint = flags.session;
   227	      ev.unstable = false;
   228	    }
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   232	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   233	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   234	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   235	  if (tail !== null) {
   236	    events.push({
   237	      type: 'transcript.set',
   238	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   239	      source: platform,
   240	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   241	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   242	    });
   243	  }
   244	
   245	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
   246	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   247	
   248	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   249	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   250	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   251	  if (unsafe === true) {
   252	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   253	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   254	  }
   255	
   256	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   257	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   258	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   259	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   260	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   261	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   262	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   263	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   264	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   265	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   266	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   267	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   268	        return false;
   269	      }
   270	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   271	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   272	      return true;
   273	    });
   274	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  let active = bundle;
   278	  let mustRewrite = false;
   279	
   280	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   281	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   282	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   283	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   284	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   285	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   286	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   287	  );
   288	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   289	
   290	  if (hasStableOwner) {
   291	    // Foreign = a different stable session, OR any platform mismatch — the
   292	    // platform is provable even when the events carry no session hint (audit
   293	    // finding: a hint-less /baton:handoff narrative from claude-code silently
   294	    // merged into — then sealed — a codex-owned bundle, corrupting the origin
   295	    // the receive-side role avoidance keys on).
   296	    const incomingLabel = stableIncoming ? stableIncoming.sessionHint : '(no session hint)';
   297	    const foreign = stableIncoming
   298	      ? stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform
   299	      : platform !== active.origin.platform;
   300	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   301	      io.stderr.write(
   302	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${incomingLabel} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   303	      );
   304	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   305	    }
   306	    if (foreign) {
   307	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   308	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   309	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   310	        io.now(),
   311	      );
   312	      if (stableIncoming) fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   313	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   314	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${incomingLabel}\n`);
   315	      active = fresh;
   316	      mustRewrite = true;
   317	    }
   318	  }
   319	
   320	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   321	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   322	  }
   323	
   324	  if (active === null) {
   325	    active = emptyBundle(
   326	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   327	      io.now(),
   328	    );
   329	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   330	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   331	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   332	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   333	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   334	    appendJournal(
   335	      root,
   336	      {
   337	        ts: io.now(),
   338	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   339	        payload: {
   340	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   341	          generation: active.generation,
   342	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   343	          origin: active.origin,
   344	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   345	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   346	        },
   347	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   348	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   349	      },
   350	      io,
   351	    );
   352	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   353	    mustRewrite = true;
   354	  }
   355	
   356	  // Ownership adoption (gate-2 fix 2): a bundle whose owner is unverified — a
   357	  // fresh seed (null hint) or a receive that defaulted to an unstable hint —
   358	  // adopts the first STABLE same-platform session as its owner, so semantic
   359	  // isolation engages from then on instead of never.
   360	  const ownerUnverified = active.origin.sessionHint === null || active.origin.unstable === true;
   361	  if (ownerUnverified && stableIncoming && active.origin.platform === platform) {
   362	    active = { ...active, origin: { ...active.origin, sessionHint: stableIncoming.sessionHint, unstable: false } };
   363	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   364	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: adopted ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} as the bundle owner (first verified session)\n`);
   365	    mustRewrite = true;
   366	  }
   367	
   368	  let lastSeq = 0;
   369	  for (const ev of events) {
   370	    const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   371	    const entry = {
   372	      ...ev,
   373	      ts,
   374	      dedupeKey:
   375	        typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0
   376	          ? ev.dedupeKey
   377	          : dedupeKey({ ts, type: ev.type, payload: ev.payload, source: ev.source, sessionHint: ev.sessionHint }),
   378	      writerId: [platform, io.pid, ev.sessionHint ?? 'local'].join('-'),
   379	    };
   380	    lastSeq = appendJournal(root, entry, io);
   381	  }
   382	
   383	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, paths.snapshot);
   384	  const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   385	  const snapUpdated = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.updatedAt === 'string' ? Date.parse(snap.value.updatedAt) : 0;
   386	  const important = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => !ROUTINE_TYPES.has(e.type));
   387	  const elapsed = Date.parse(io.now()) - snapUpdated > THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS;
   388	  const accreted = lastSeq - snapSeq;
   389	
   390	  let rewritten = false;
   391	  if (mustRewrite || important || elapsed || accreted >= THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT) {
   392	    // Bounded git refresh on EVERY snapshot rewrite (gate-2 iter-2 M1): a
   393	    // mechanical checkpoint carries branch/HEAD/dirty. Capture git BEFORE the
   394	    // lock — it is async and must not straddle the lock — then do ONE atomic
   395	    // read-modify-write UNDER the lock (iter-5 A1): reload the bundle inside the
   396	    // lock, apply git, and write with the fencing token. The old code loaded
   397	    // outside the lock, awaited git (yielding the event loop), then wrote the
   398	    // stale bundle, so a concurrent --take-over during the await was clobbered
   399	    // (origin rolled back, the new owner's event lost). Reloading inside the
   400	    // lock guarantees the write is built from current state.
   401	    const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   402	    rewritten = withLock(root, io, (token) => {
   403	      const merged = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
   404	      if (!merged) return false;
   405	      if (git !== null) {
   406	        merged.git = git;
   407	      } else {
   408	        // git refresh failed/timed out (iter-4 I2/F5): NEVER retain the prior
   409	        // snapshot — stale HEAD/dirty presented as current corrupts the
   410	        // receive-side evidence audit. Clear to explicit unavailable (null) and
   411	        // warn; a repo that was never git (already null) warns nothing.
   412	        if (merged.git !== null) {
   413	          io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: git refresh failed — clearing stale git state (marked unavailable; receive re-derives it live)\n');
   414	        }
   415	        merged.git = null;
   416	      }
   417	      writeSnapshotIn(root, merged, io, token);
   418	      return true;
   419	    });
   420	  }
   421	  return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: events.length, rewritten } }, 0);
   422	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { hostname } from 'node:os';
     2	
     3	// Best-effort extraction of baton events from raw harness hook payloads.
     4	// Total: any input value yields an Event[] — garbage degrades to note events,
     5	// never a throw, so a checkpoint can never break the host harness.
     6	
     7	/** @typedef {{host: string, pid: number, startTime: number | null}} SessionFacts */
     8	
     9	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    10	const FILE_TOOL_OPS = { Write: 'write', Edit: 'edit' };
    11	
    12	/** @returns {SessionFacts} */
    13	function processFacts() {
    14	  return { host: hostname(), pid: process.pid, startTime: Math.round(performance.timeOrigin) };
    15	}
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * A process-scoped hint for payloads lacking a stable session id. Deterministic
    19	 * over the identity facts: one process => one hint; unstable hints never
    20	 * trigger foreign-session rejection on their own (enforced at the command).
    21	 * @param {SessionFacts | undefined} session
    22	 */
    23	function generatedHint(session) {
    24	  const f = session ?? processFacts();
    25	  return ['unstable', f.host, f.pid, f.startTime].join(':');
    26	}
    27	
    28	/**
    29	 * @param {any} raw
    30	 * @param {SessionFacts | undefined} session
    31	 * @returns {{sessionHint: string, unstable: boolean}}
    32	 */
    33	function deriveHint(raw, session) {
    34	  // Cursor identifies the session as conversation_id (audit finding 26): with
    35	  // only session_id read, every cursor hook fire fell back to a per-process
    36	  // unstable hint — a warning on every invocation and ownership adoption that
    37	  // never engaged.
    38	  const id = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.session_id ?? raw.conversation_id) : undefined;
    39	  if (typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0) return { sessionHint: id, unstable: false };
    40	  return { sessionHint: generatedHint(session), unstable: true };
    41	}
    42	
    43	/**
    44	 * Normalize a parsed hook payload into baton/event@1 events. Adapters that
    45	 * already speak baton/event@1 pass through verbatim; raw per-harness payloads
    46	 * get best-effort extraction; unknowns degrade to note events. Never throws.
    47	 * @param {any} raw parsed JS value (the caller owns JSON.parse)
    48	 * @param {string} platform 'claude-code' | 'codex' | 'cursor'
    49	 * @param {SessionFacts} [session] identity facts for generated hints; defaults to this process
    50	 * @param {string} [explicitEvent] the hook event name passed on the command line
    51	 *   (`--trigger`). Authoritative when present — the hooks manifest already keys
    52	 *   each command by event, so this needs no per-harness payload parsing. Cursor's
    53	 *   stop payload carries neither `hook_event_name` nor `event`, so without this
    54	 *   its checkpoint degraded to trigger 'unknown' and never satisfied the canary.
    55	 * @returns {any[]}
    56	 */
    57	export function normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent) {
    58	  // Passthrough tier: the adapter formed the events; return them untouched.
    59	  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && raw.schema === 'baton/event@1') {
    60	    if (Array.isArray(raw.events)) return raw.events;
    61	    return [raw];
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  const { sessionHint, unstable } = deriveHint(raw, session);
    65	  /** @param {string} type @param {Record<string, any>} payload */
    66	  const event = (type, payload) => ({ type, payload, source: platform, sessionHint, unstable });
    67	  /** @param {string} trigger */
    68	  const note = (trigger) => event('note', { trigger, text: 'hook ' + trigger + ' observed on ' + platform });
    69	
    70	  const eventName =
    71	    (typeof explicitEvent === 'string' && explicitEvent.length > 0 && explicitEvent) ||
    72	    (raw && typeof raw === 'object'
    73	      ? (typeof raw.hook_event_name === 'string' && raw.hook_event_name) ||
    74	        (platform === 'cursor' && typeof raw.event === 'string' && raw.event) ||
    75	        null
    76	      : null);
    77	  if (!eventName) return [note('unknown')];
    78	
    79	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
    80	    if (eventName === 'PostToolUse') {
    81	      const op = FILE_TOOL_OPS[raw.tool_name];
    82	      const path = raw.tool_input && typeof raw.tool_input === 'object' ? raw.tool_input.file_path : undefined;
    83	      if (op && typeof path === 'string') return [event('file.touch', { path, op })];
    84	      return [note('PostToolUse')];
    85	    }
    86	    if (eventName === 'StopFailure') {
    87	      const errorType = raw.error && typeof raw.error === 'object' && typeof raw.error.type === 'string' ? raw.error.type : 'unknown';
    88	      return [
    89	        event('note', {
    90	          trigger: 'StopFailure',
    91	          text: 'turn ended on API error: ' + errorType,
    92	          errorType,
    93	          structured: { kind: 'stop-failure', errorType },
    94	        }),
    95	      ];
    96	    }
    97	    return [note(eventName)];
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  // Codex and Cursor (and any future platform string): every lifecycle event
   101	  // is a note carrying its trigger; the harness is the source.
   102	  return [note(eventName)];
   103	}

Verified: `node --test tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs` is RED with 14/14 failures through the `M()` import guard; the guard targets `core/src/loop/failover.mjs` at [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:37). `npm run typecheck` passed.

Assessment: the suite is directionally good, but not yet adversarial enough for implementation. The biggest gaps are that checkpoint-before-seal/finalize is not actually pinned, usage-limit platform avoidance can pass accidentally, and the stale-retry cap is not counted.

VERDICT: BLOCKED
FINDINGS:
1. [high] Checkpoint-before-seal/finalize is not falsifiable: the test searches frozen JSON for `/run1/`, but the seed already contains `origin.sessionHint: 'loop-run1'`, so an implementation can skip the loop-position checkpoint and still pass; it also does not require a `.finalize.json` freeze or `handoff.status === 'sealed'` despite finalize writing a sealed snapshot then rotating history. See [test seed](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:82), [assertion](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:246), and finalize rotation [core/src/commands/finalize.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/finalize.mjs:83). Fix: assert a `*.finalize.json` history artifact with `status:'sealed'`, `reasonClass:'usage-limit'`, and a structured loop-position field not present in the seed.
2. [high] Usage-limit relaunch does not prove dead-platform avoidance: the tested `implementer` chain starts with `claude-code`, while the dead assignment is `codex`, so forgetting `avoid:['codex']` still selects a non-codex target. See config chain [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:67) and assertion [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:293). Fix: add a usage-limit fixture using `plan-reviewer` or a local role whose first entry is the dead `codex` platform and second entry is non-codex.
3. [medium] Repeated-staleness retry cap is not asserted: the test parks on repeated drift but does not assert exactly two prepare→commit windows, so a bounded three-or-more retry implementation could still pass. See drift hook counter setup [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:321). Fix: assert the drift injector/window counter is exactly `2` on the second-stale park path.
4. [medium] Classifier delegation is not pinned against the injected signature table: the suite seeds signatures, but uses the shipped literal strings, so hardcoded substring handling could pass without loading/classifying through the table. See signature seeding [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:49) and classifier reset-hint behavior [core/src/detect/classifier.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/detect/classifier.mjs:72). Fix: add a fixture-only signature table entry and transcript that only passes if `runFailover` consults `loadSignatures`/`classify`.
5. [medium] `hooks.beforeCommit()` is a weak test-only seam for real drift: both stale tests depend on the hook rather than drift arising from the injected IO that production already uses. See hook API [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:24), hook use [tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/loop-failover.test.mjs:314), and real git snapshot IO [core/src/git/snapshot.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/git/snapshot.mjs:27). Fix: express drift through `io.execFile` call sequencing, e.g. `rev-parse HEAD` returns different values between prepare and commit.
